CLASS "imad__RCR_RCR"
FORMAT PREDICATE @[!]Predicate(PT):Pg Opcode /LOOnly("LO"):wide /FMT("S32"):fmt
Register:Rd
','Register:Ra {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_a}
','C:Sb[UImm(5/0*):Sb_bank]*   [SImm(17)*:Sb_addr]
',' [-] Register:Rc {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_c}
$( { '&' REQ:req '=' BITSET(6/0x0000):req_bit_set } )$
$( { '?' USCHED_INFO("DRAIN"):usched_info } )$
$( { '?' BATCH_T("NOP"):batch_t } )$
$( { '?' PM_PRED("PMN"):pm_pred } )$;

CONDITIONS

OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rd)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rd)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Rd)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rd is out of range"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Ra)==`Register@RZ)||(((Ra)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Ra)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Ra is out of range"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((Sb_bank <= 17) || (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31)) :
 "Invalid constant bank error"
MISALIGNED_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_addr & 0x3) == 0 :
 "Constant offsets must be aligned on a 4B boundary"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31) -> (Sb_addr <= 255) :
 "RTV banks may not use an offset greater than 255"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 (%SHADER_TYPE == $ST_CS) -> !(Sb_bank >= 8 && Sb_bank <= 31) :
 "CS may not use RTV banks"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rc)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Rc)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rc is out of range"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 DEFINED TABLES_opex_1(batch_t,usched_info,reuse_src_a,reuse_src_c) :
 "Invalid combination of batch_t, usched_info, reuse_src_a, reuse_src_c"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((reuse_src_a==1)||(reuse_src_c==1)) -> ((usched_info==17)||(usched_info==18)||(usched_info==19)||(usched_info==20)||(usched_info==21)||(usched_info==22)||(usched_info==23)||(usched_info==24)||(usched_info==25)||(usched_info==26)||(usched_info==27)) :
 "?DRAIN and ?WAITn_END_GROUP tokens are not allowed with .reuse"


PROPERTIES
 INSTRUCTION_TYPE = INST_TYPE_COUPLED_MATH;
 MIN_WAIT_NEEDED = 0 ;
 SIDL_NAME = `SIDL_NAMES@IMAD_C_Rc ;

        IMAD =  0b101000100100;

BITS_1_15_15_Pg_not = Pg@not;

BITS_1_73_73_sz=fmt;
BITS_1_74_74_Sc_absolute=0;
BITS_8_23_16_Rd=Rd;
BITS_3_83_81_Pu=*7;
BITS_8_31_24_Ra=Ra;

BITS_5_58_54_Sc_bank,BITS_14_53_40_Sc_addr =  ConstBankAddress2(Sb_bank,Sb_addr);
BITS_8_71_64_Rc=Rc;
BITS_1_75_75_Sc_negate=Rc@negate;
BITS_3_89_87_Pp =* 7;
BITS_1_90_90_input_reg_sz_32_dist =*1;

','C:Sb[UImm(5/0*):Sb_bank]*   [SImm(17)*:Sb_addr]
',' [-] Register:Rc {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_c}
$( { '&' REQ:req '=' BITSET(6/0x0000):req_bit_set } )$

 "Register Ra is out of range"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((Sb_bank <= 17) || (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31)) :
 "Invalid constant bank error"
MISALIGNED_ADDR_ERROR

 "Constant offsets must be aligned on a 4B boundary"

 "RTV banks may not use an offset greater than 255"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 (%SHADER_TYPE == $ST_CS) -> !(Sb_bank >= 8 && Sb_bank <= 31) :
 "CS may not use RTV banks"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rc)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-2))&&((Rc)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rc is out of range"
MISALIGNED_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)+((Rc)==`Register@RZ)) % 2) == 0 :
 "Register Rc is misaligned"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 DEFINED TABLES_opex_1(batch_t,usched_info,reuse_src_a,reuse_src_c) :

ALTERNATE CLASS "imad_pseudo__RCR_RCR"
FORMAT PREDICATE @[!]Predicate(PT):Pg Opcode /LOOnly("LO"):wide /PSEUDO_OPCODE("nopseudo_opcode"):pseudo_opcode /FMT("S32"):fmt
Register:Rd
','Register:Ra {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_a}
','C:Sb[UImm(5/0*):Sb_bank]*   [SImm(17)*:Sb_addr]
',' [-] Register:Rc {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_c}
$( { '&' REQ:req '=' BITSET(6/0x0000):req_bit_set } )$
$( { '?' USCHED_INFO("DRAIN"):usched_info } )$
$( { '?' BATCH_T("NOP"):batch_t } )$
$( { '?' PM_PRED("PMN"):pm_pred } )$;


FORMAT_ALIAS pseudo_opcode = PSEUDO_OPCODE1:GetPseudoOpRCR(Ra, Sb, Rc);

CONDITIONS

OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rd)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rd)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Rd)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rd is out of range"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Ra)==`Register@RZ)||(((Ra)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Ra)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Ra is out of range"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((Sb_bank <= 17) || (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31)) :
 "Invalid constant bank error"
MISALIGNED_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_addr & 0x3) == 0 :
 "Constant offsets must be aligned on a 4B boundary"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31) -> (Sb_addr <= 255) :
 "RTV banks may not use an offset greater than 255"

','Register:Ra {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_a}
','C:Sb[UImm(5/0*):Sb_bank]*   [SImm(17)*:Sb_addr]
',' [-] Register:Rc {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_c}

$( { '?' USCHED_INFO("DRAIN"):usched_info } )$
$( { '?' BATCH_T("NOP"):batch_t } )$
$( { '?' PM_PRED("PMN"):pm_pred } )$;

 (((Rd)+((Rd)==`Register@RZ)) % 2) == 0 :
 "Register Rd is misaligned"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Ra)==`Register@RZ)||(((Ra)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Ra)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Ra is out of range"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((Sb_bank <= 17) || (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31)) :
 "Invalid constant bank error"
MISALIGNED_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_addr & 0x3) == 0 :
 "Constant offsets must be aligned on a 4B boundary"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sb_bank >= 24 && Sb_bank <= 31) -> (Sb_addr <= 255) :
 "RTV banks may not use an offset greater than 255"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 (%SHADER_TYPE == $ST_CS) -> !(Sb_bank >= 8 && Sb_bank <= 31) :
 "CS may not use RTV banks"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rc)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-2))&&((Rc)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rc is out of range"
MISALIGNED_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)+((Rc)==`Register@RZ)) % 2) == 0 :
 "Register Rc is misaligned"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 DEFINED TABLES_opex_1(batch_t,usched_info,reuse_src_a,reuse_src_c) :

INVALID_CONST_ADDR_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((Sc_bank <= 17) || (Sc_bank >= 24 && Sc_bank <= 31)) :
 "Invalid constant bank error"
MISALIGNED_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sc_addr & 0x3) == 0 :
 "Constant offsets must be aligned on a 4B boundary"
INVALID_CONST_ADDR_ERROR
 (Sc_bank >= 24 && Sc_bank <= 31) -> (Sc_addr <= 255) :
 "RTV banks may not use an offset greater than 255"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 (%SHADER_TYPE == $ST_CS) -> !(Sc_bank >= 8 && Sc_bank <= 31) :
 "CS may not use RTV banks"

',' [~] Register:Rc {/REUSE("noreuse"):reuse_src_c}
',' [!]Predicate:Pp
$( { '&' REQ:req '=' BITSET(6/0x0000):req_bit_set } )$
$( { '?' USCHED_INFO("DRAIN"):usched_info } )$
$( { '?' BATCH_T("NOP"):batch_t } )$
$( { '?' PM_PRED("PMN"):pm_pred } )$;

CONDITIONS

OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rd)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rd)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Rd)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rd is out of range"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Ra)==`Register@RZ)||(((Ra)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Ra)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Ra is out of range"
OOR_REG_ERROR
 (((Rc)==`Register@RZ)||(((Rc)<=(%MAX_REG_COUNT-1))&&((Rc)!=`Register@R254))) :
 "Register Rc is out of range"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 DEFINED TABLES_opex_1(batch_t,usched_info,reuse_src_a,reuse_src_c) :
 "Invalid combination of batch_t, usched_info, reuse_src_a, reuse_src_c"
ILLEGAL_INSTR_ENCODING_SASS_ONLY_ERROR
 ((reuse_src_a==1)||(reuse_src_c==1)) -> ((usched_info==17)||(usched_info==18)||(usched_info==19)||(usched_info==20)||(usched_info==21)||(usched_info==22)||(usched_info==23)||(usched_info==24)||(usched_info==25)||(usched_info==26)||(usched_info==27)) :
 "?DRAIN and ?WAITn_END_GROUP tokens are not allowed with .reuse"
